#Hey, wanna some Net Protectorate - Question
country_event = {
	id = sov_nabi.1
	title = sov_nabi.1.t
	desc = sov_nabi.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_sov_event_techoprotectorate
	option = {
		name = sov_nabi.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nabi.1.a"
		SOV = {
			country_event = { id = sov_nabi.2 }
		}
		add_ideas = SOV_elvire_net_protectorate_idea
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_positive }
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_positive }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				sender_influence_higher_5 = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				sender_influence_higher_15 = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 5
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 15
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 5
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 50
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 100
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sov_nabi.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nabi.1.b"
		SOV = {
			country_event = { id = sov_nabi.3 }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_negative }
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_negative }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				sender_influence_higher_30 = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value < -15
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 30
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value < -50
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 50
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value < -100
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Putin Content
#Kosak-Memorandum - Choose Destiny
country_event = {
	id = sov_putin.1
	title = sov_putin.1.t
	desc = sov_putin.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_sov_kosak_memorandum
	option = {
		name = sov_putin.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_putin.1.a"
		SOV = {
			country_event = { id = sov_putin.2 }
		}
		set_autonomy = {
			target = PMR
			autonomy_state = autonomy_autonomous_state
		}
		GGZ = {
			transfer_state = 997
			add_state_core = 997
		}
		set_autonomy = {
			target = GGZ
			autonomy_state = autonomy_autonomous_state
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_positive }
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_positive }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				OR = {
					check_variable = { influence_array^0 = SOV } 
					check_variable = { influence_array^1 = SOV } 
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				has_opinion = { target = SOV value > 150 }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				has_opinion = { target = SOV value > 50 }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 200
				is_subject_of = SOV 
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 200
				is_in_faction_with = SOV
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				is_guaranteed_by = SOV
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sov_putin.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_putin.1.b"
		SOV = {
			country_event = { id = sov_putin.3 }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_negative }
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = recent_actions_negative }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				has_opinion = { target = SOV value < 50 }
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				OR = {
					check_variable = { influence_array^2 = SOV } 
					check_variable = { influence_array^3 = SOV }
					check_variable = { influence_array^4 = SOV }  
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				NOT = {
					is_in_faction_with = SOV
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 20
				NOT = {
					is_subject_of = SOV 
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#Demand Belarus' Annexation/Puppeting
country_event = {
	id = russia.10
	title = {
		trigger = { BLR = { is_subject_of = SOV } }
		text = russia.10.t1
	}
	title = {
		trigger = { BLR = { NOT = { is_subject_of = SOV } } }
		text = russia.10.t2
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { BLR = { is_subject_of = SOV } }
		text = russia.10.d1
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { BLR = { NOT = { is_subject_of = SOV } } }
		text = russia.10.d2
	}
	picture = GFX_SOV_generic

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = russia.10.o1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.10.o1 executed"
		if = {
			limit = { BLR = { is_subject_of = SOV } }
			effect_tooltip = {
				BLR = {
					every_owned_state = {
						add_core_of = SOV
					}
				}
				annex_country = { target = BLR transfer_troops = yes }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { BLR = { NOT = { is_subject_of = SOV } } }
			effect_tooltip = {
				BLR = {
					every_owned_state = {
						add_core_of = SOV
					}
				}
				SOV = { puppet = BLR }
			}
		}
		SOV = {
			country_event = { id = russia.3 days = 1 }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
		}
	}

	#Belarus Refuses!
	option = {
		name = russia.10.o2
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.10.o3 executed"
		if = {
			limit = { BLR = { is_subject_of = SOV } }
			SOV = { release_puppet = BLR }
			BLR = {
				every_owned_state = {
					add_core_of = SOV
				}
			}
			SOV = {
				create_wargoal = {
					target = BLR
					type = take_state_focus
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { BLR = { NOT = { is_subject_of = SOV } } }
			BLR = {
				every_owned_state = {
					add_core_of = SOV
				}
			}
			SOV = {
				create_wargoal = {
					target = BLR
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
		}
		SOV = { add_opinion_modifier = { target = BLR modifier = betrayed_our_cause } }
		SOV = { country_event = { id = russia.4 days = 1 } }

		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
}

##Annex Abkhazia and South Ossetia!
country_event = {
	id = russia.11
	title = russia.11.t
	desc = russia.11.d
	picture = GFX_southern_options
	is_triggered_only = yes

	##Accept the Integration
	option = {
		name = russia.11.o1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.11.o1 executed"
		if = {
			limit = { tag = ABK }
			SOV = { set_country_flag = ABK_accepts_annexation }
			effect_tooltip = {
				ABK = {
					every_unit_leader = {
						set_nationality = SOV
					}
				}
				SOV = { annex_country = { target = ABK transfer_troops = yes } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SOO }
			SOV = { set_country_flag = SOO_accepts_annexation }
			effect_tooltip = {
				SOO = {
					every_unit_leader = {
						set_nationality = SOV
					}
				}
				SOV = { annex_country = { target = SOO transfer_troops = yes } }
			}
		}
		SOV = { country_event = { id = russia.12 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				SOV_targeted_country_has_more_than_10_percent_influence = yes
				add = 5
			}
			modifier = {
				SOV_targeted_country_has_more_than_20_percent_influence = yes
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				SOV_targeted_country_has_more_than_30_percent_influence = yes
				add = 30
			}
		}
	}
	#Refuse the Annexation
	option = {
		name = russia.11.o2
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.11.o2 executed"
		if = {
			limit = { tag = ABK }
			SOV = {
				set_country_flag = ABK_refused_russias_annexation
				create_wargoal = {
					target = ABK
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
			SOV = { country_event = { id = russia.13 days = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = SOO }
			SOV = {
				set_country_flag = SOO_refused_russias_annexation
				create_wargoal = {
					target = SOO
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
			SOV = { country_event = { id = russia.13 days = 1 } }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
}

##Abkhazia/South Ossetia Accepts
country_event = {
	id = russia.12
	title = {
		trigger = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = ABK_accepts_annexation }
		}
		text = russia.12.t1
	}
	title = {
		trigger = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = SOO_accepts_annexation }
		}
		text = russia.12.t2
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = ABK_accepts_annexation }
		}
		text = russia.12.d1
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = SOO_accepts_annexation }
		}
		text = russia.12.d2
	}
	picture = GFX_southern_options
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = russia.12.o1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.12.o1 executed"
		if = {
			limit = { SOV = { has_country_flag = ABK_accepts_annexation } }
			ABK = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_owned_state = { NOT = { is_core_of = SOV } } }
					every_owned_state = {
						add_core_of = SOV
					}
				}
				every_unit_leader = {
					set_nationality = SOV
				}
			}
			SOV = { annex_country = { target = ABK transfer_troops = yes } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SOV = { has_country_flag = SOO_accepts_annexation } }
			SOO = {
				if = {
					limit = { any_owned_state = { NOT = { is_core_of = SOV } } }
					every_owned_state = {
						add_core_of = SOV
					}
				}
				every_unit_leader = {
					set_nationality = SOV
				}
			}
			SOV = { annex_country = { target = SOO transfer_troops = yes } }
			clr_country_flag = SOO_accepts_annexation
		}
	}
}

###Russian Investments
country_event = {
	id = russia.14
	title = russia.14.t
	desc = russia.14.d
	picture = GFX_kiev_construction
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	#Accepts
	option = {
		name = russia.14.o1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.14.o1 executed"
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
		}
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV.id }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = THIS.id }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		SOV = {
			set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 10 }
			modify_international_investment_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -10 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			country_event = { id = diplomatic_response.1 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				SOV_targeted_country_has_more_than_10_percent_influence = yes
				add = 5
			}
			modifier = {
				SOV_targeted_country_has_more_than_20_percent_influence = yes
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				SOV_targeted_country_has_more_than_30_percent_influence = yes
				add = 15
			}
		}
	}

	#Refuse
	option = {
		name = russia.14.o2
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.14.o2 executed"

		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = -5 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV.id }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = THIS.id }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = diplomatic_distance }
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = SOV modifier = diplomatic_distance }
		SOV = { country_event = { id = diplomatic_response.2 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {

##Annex Alaska
country_event = {
	id = russia.21
	title = russia.21.t
	desc = russia.21.d
	picture = GFX_SOV_generic
	is_triggered_only = yes

	##Accept the Annexation
	option = {
		name = russia.21.o1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.21.o1 executed"
		if = {
			limit = { tag = USA }
			SOV = { set_country_flag = USA_accepts_annexation }
			effect_tooltip = {
				SOV = {
					transfer_state = 814
					add_state_core = 814
				}
			}
		}
		SOV = { country_event = { id = russia.22 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
	#Refuse the Annexation
	option = {
		name = russia.21.o2
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.21.o2 executed"
		if = {
			limit = { tag = USA }
			SOV = { set_country_flag = USA_refused_annexation }
			SOV = {
				add_state_core = 814
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = USA
					modifier = major_breach_of_trust
				}
			}
			SOV = { country_event = { id = russia.23 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
	}
}

#Referendum in Chechnya
country_event = {
	id = russia.29
	title = russia.29.t
	desc = russia.29.d
	picture = GFX_referendum_chechnya
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = russia.29.o1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.29.o2 executed"
		CHE = {
			set_country_flag = GENERIC_autonomy_federation_flag
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { country_exists = CHE } }
			release_autonomy = {
				target = CHE
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet_federation
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = CHE
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet_federation
			}
		}
		add_state_core = 672
		hidden_effect = {
			CHE = {
				set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { change_leader_temp = 1 }
				change_ruling_party_effect = yes
				if = {
					limit = { CHE = { is_ai = yes } }
					set_temp_variable = { party_index = 6 }
					set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.35 }
					set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.35 }
					add_relative_party_popularity = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = russia.29.o2
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.29.o2 executed"
		CHE = {
			set_country_flag = GENERIC_autonomy_federation_flag
			change_tag_from = SOV
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { country_exists = CHE } }
			release_autonomy = {
				target = CHE
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet_federation
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = CHE
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet_federation
			}
		}
		add_state_core = 672
		hidden_effect = {
			CHE = {
				set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 6 }
				set_temp_variable = { change_leader_temp = 1 }
				change_ruling_party_effect = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SOO }
			annex_country = { target = SOO transfer_troops = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = russia.50.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.50.b executed"
		GEO = {
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ABK }
			annex_country = { target = ABK transfer_troops = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = SOO }
			annex_country = { target = SOO transfer_troops = yes }
		}
		}
	}
}

	id = russia.118
	title = russia.118.t
	desc = russia.118.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_SOV_generic
	option = {
		name = russia.118.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.118.a executed"
		custom_effect_tooltip = SOV_baku_not_sell_tt
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -6 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			create_equipment_variant = {
					name = "Kiev-class"
					type = carrier_hull_2
					name_group = SOV_CV_HISTORICAL
					parent_version = 0
					modules = {
						fixed_ship_engine_slot = module_surface_oil_power
						fixed_ship_fire_control_system_slot = module_digital_integrated_fire_control
						fixed_ship_radar_slot = module_radar_3
						fixed_ship_auxillary_slot_2 = module_ciws_2
						fixed_ship_auxillary_slot_1 = module_ciws_2
						fixed_ship_auxillary_slot = module_ciws_2
						fixed_ship_flight_deck = module_flight_deck_2
						front_1_custom_slot = module_vls_asm_2
						front_2_custom_slot = module_vls_sam_2
						mid_1_custom_slot = module_vls_sam_2
						mid_2_custom_slot = module_helipads_1
					}
				}
				set_technology = { carrier_hull_2 = 1 }
				load_oob = "SOV_naval_baku_MTG"
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = russia.118.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia.118.b executed"
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = 6 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

	}

	id = russia_news.7
	title = russia_news.7.t
	desc = russia_news.7.d
	picture = GFX_trade_agreement
	major = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = russia_news.7.o1
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia_news.7.o1 executed"
		trigger = { original_tag = SOV }
	}

	option = {
		name = russia_news.7.o2
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia_news.7.o2 executed"
		trigger = {
			is_in_faction_with = SOV
			NOT = { original_tag = SOV }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = russia_news.7.o3
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia_news.7.o3 executed"
		trigger = {
			NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SOV }
			NOT = { original_tag = SOV }
			NOT = { has_war_with = SOV }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = russia_news.7.o4
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: russia_news.7.o4 executed"
		trigger = {
			has_war_with = SOV
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = sov_vybory.1
	title = sov_vybory.1.t
	desc = sov_vybory.1.d
	picture = GFX_sov_dima_pobeda
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = SOV }
	option = {
		name = sov_vybory.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_vybory.1.a executed"
		add_to_array = { ruling_party = 6 }
		update_government_coalition_strength = yes
		create_country_leader = {
			name = "Dmitriy Medvedev"
			picture = "sov_dimochka.dds"
			ideology = Conservative
			traits = {
				emerging_Conservative
				political_dancer
				western_liberalism
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = sov_vybory.2
	title = sov_vybory.2.t
	desc = sov_vybory.2.d
	picture = GFX_vladimir_putin
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { tag = SOV }
	option = {
		name = sov_vybory.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_vybory.1.a executed"
		retire_country_leader = yes
		create_country_leader = {
			name = "Vladimir V.Putin"
			picture = "Vladimir_Putin_older.dds"
			ideology = Conservative
			traits = {
				spy emerging_Conservative geopolitical_thinker political_dancer likeable sly rational
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { rul_party_temp = 6 }
 		set_temp_variable = { col_one = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { col_two = 15 }
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = communism
			elections_allowed = yes
			election_frequency = 72
		}

		add_stability = -0.1
		country_event = { id = rusboloto.1 days = 14 }
	}
}

	desc = rusboloto.1.d

	trigger = { tag = SOV }

	option = {
		name = rusboloto.1.a

		add_stability = 0.05
		country_event = { id = rusboloto.2 days = 5 }
	}

	option = {
		name = rusboloto.2.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_vybory.1.a executed"
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^20 = 0.05 }
			recalculate_party = yes
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = democratic
				popularity = 0.05
			}
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^4 = 0.05 }
			recalculate_party = yes
		}
		hold_election = SOV
	}
}

			}

			}

				add = 10
				sender_influence_higher_40 = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 50
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 100
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				has_government = nationalist
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sov_other.13.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_vybory.1.a executed"
		SOV = {
			add_state_core = 1076

country_event = {
	id = sov_communists.7
	title = sov_communists.7.t
	desc = sov_communists.7.d
	picture = GFX_communism
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = sov_communists.7.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_communists.7.a executed"
		ROOT = {
			every_owned_state = {
				add_core_of = SOV
			}
		}
		SOV = {
			add_to_variable = { treasury = ROOT.treasury }
			clamp_variable = { var = treasury min = -1000000 max = 1000000 }
			add_to_variable = { debt = ROOT.debt }
	    	clamp_variable = { var = debt min = 0 max = 1000000 }
			add_to_variable = { int_investments = ROOT.int_investments }
    		clamp_variable = { var = int_investments min = 0 max = 1000000 }
			annex_country = { target = ROOT transfer_troops = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sov_communists.7.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_communists.7.b executed"
		SOV = {
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = betrayed_our_cause }
			create_wargoal = {
				type = puppet_wargoal_focus
				target = ROOT
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

					value > 15
				}

				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 50
				}
			}

				add = 10

					target = SOV
					value > 100
				}
			}
		}

			}
		}

				has_government = neutrality
				factor = 50
			}

#Annex Manchuria
country_event = {
	id = sov_nationalists.10
	title = sov_nationalists.10.t
	desc = sov_nationalists.10.d
	picture = GFX_sov_empire_herb
	is_triggered_only = yes

	##Accept the Annexation
	option = {
		name = sov_nationalists.10.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nationalists.10.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { tag = CHI }
			SOV = { set_country_flag = CHI_accepts_annexation }
			effect_tooltip = {
				SOV = { transfer_state = 1097 }
				SOV = { transfer_state = 555 }
				SOV = { transfer_state = 1096 }
				SOV = { transfer_state = 554 }
				SOV = { transfer_state = 552 }
				SOV = { transfer_state = 1095 }
			}
		}
		SOV = { country_event = { id = sov_nationalists.11 days = 1 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = {
				strength_ratio = {
					tag = SOV
					ratio > 1
				}
				add = 75
			}
		}
	}
	#Refuse the Annexation
	option = {
		name = sov_nationalists.10.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nationalists.10.b executed"
		if = {
			limit = { tag = CHI }
			SOV = {
				set_country_flag = CHI_refused_annexation
				create_wargoal = {
					target = CHI
					type = take_claimed_state
				}
			}
			SOV = { country_event = { id = sov_nationalists.12 days = 1 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = {
				strength_ratio = {
					tag = SOV
					ratio < 1
				}
				add = 75
			}
		}
	}
}
#China Accepts
country_event = {
	id = sov_nationalists.11
	title = {
		trigger = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = CHI_accepts_annexation }
		}
		text = sov_nationalists.11.t
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			SOV = { has_country_flag = CHI_accepts_annexation }
		}
		text = sov_nationalists.11.d
	}
	picture = GFX_sov_empire_herb
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = sov_nationalists.11.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nationalists.11.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { SOV = { has_country_flag = CHI_accepts_annexation } }
			SOV = { transfer_state = 1097 }
			SOV = { transfer_state = 555 }
			SOV = { transfer_state = 1096 }
		}
	}
}

		}
		text = sov_nationalists.12.t
	}
	picture = GFX_sov_empire_herb
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = sov_nationalists.12.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nationalists.12.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { tag = CHI } }
			effect_tooltip = {
				create_wargoal = {
					target = CHI
					type = take_claimed_state
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
#Abkhazia and South Ossetia
country_event = {
	id = sov_nationalists.13
	title = sov_nationalists.13.t
	desc = sov_nationalists.13.d
	picture = GFX_sov_empire_herb
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = sov_nationalists.13.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nationalists.13.a executed"
		ROOT = {
			every_owned_state = {
				add_core_of = SOV
			}
		}
		SOV = {
			country_event = sov_nationalists.19
			add_to_variable = { treasury = ROOT.treasury }
			clamp_variable = { var = treasury min = -1000000 max = 1000000 }
			add_to_variable = { debt = ROOT.debt }
	    	clamp_variable = { var = debt min = 0 max = 1000000 }
			add_to_variable = { int_investments = ROOT.int_investments }
    		clamp_variable = { var = int_investments min = 0 max = 1000000 }
			annex_country = { target = ROOT transfer_troops = yes }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sov_nationalists.13.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_nationalists.13.b executed"
		SOV = {
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = betrayed_our_cause }
			create_wargoal = {
				type = puppet_wargoal_focus
				target = ROOT
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

##Impose sanctions on Russia
country_event = {
	id = sov_final_strike.4
	title = sov_final_strike.4.t
	desc = sov_final_strike.4.d
	picture = GFX_SOV_generic
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = sov_final_strike.4.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_final_strike.4.a"
		set_country_flag = SOV_imposed_sanctions
		SOV = {
			add_to_variable = { SOV_sanctions_roi = -0.02 }
			add_to_variable = { SOV_sanctions_invest_cost = 0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { SOV_sanctions_office_tax_gain = -0.02 }
			add_to_variable = { SOV_sanctions_construction_speed = -0.02 }
			add_to_variable = { SOV_sanctions_opinion_gain = -0.01 }
			add_to_variable = { SOV_sanctions_research_speed = -0.01 }
			add_to_variable = { SOV_sanctions_tax_cost = 0.01 }
			set_country_flag = SOV_has_santions_flag
			country_event = { id = sov_final_strike.5 days = 1 }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				add = 70
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sov_final_strike.4.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_final_strike.4.b"
		UKR = {
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = ROOT
				modifier = major_breach_of_trust
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}
	}
}

##Operation Declared in Finland
country_event = {
	id = sov_final_strike.8
	title = sov_final_strike.8.t
	desc = sov_final_strike.8.d
	picture = GFX_declare_svo
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = { has_completed_focus = SOV_secret_strike }
	option = {
		name = sov_final_strike.8.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_final_strike.8.a"
		add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^6 = 0.05 }
		recalculate_party = yes
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_idea = NATO_member
					has_idea = EU_member
				}
			}
			add_opinion_modifier = {
				target = SOV
				modifier = major_breach_of_trust
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			news_event = {
				hours = 6
				id = russia_news.13
			}
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						OR = {
							has_idea = negligible_corruption
							has_idea = slight_corruption
							has_idea = modest_corruption
							has_idea = medium_corruption
						}
					}
				}
				SOV = {
					country_event = { id = sov_final_strike.2 days = 15 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				SOV = {
					country_event = { id = sov_final_strike.3 days = 15 }
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
		}
	}
}

			set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = party_pop_array^21 }
			add_relative_party_popularity = yes
			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 15 }
			add_to_array = { gov_coalition_array = 16 }

			update_party_name = yes
			set_coalition_drift = yes
			set_ruling_leader = yes
			set_leader = yes
			remove_ideas = idea_SOV_oligarchic_republic
		}

			name = "Vladimir Putin"
			picture = "oligarchic_putin.dds"
			ideology = oligarchism
			traits = { spy geopolitical_thinker political_dancer likeable sly rational corruptible }

			base = 50
		}
	}

	desc = sov_economic.16.d

	option = {
		name = sov_economic.16.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_economic.16.a executed"
		add_ideas = SOV_azot_fertilizers_idea
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SOV
			modifier = trade_mission
		}
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = SOV
			modifier = trade_mission
		}
		SOV = {
			country_event = { id = sov_economic.17 days = 1 }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1

			modifier = {
				THIS = { has_opinion = { target = SOV value > -20 } }
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				is_top5_influencer = yes
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sov_economic.16.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_economic.16.b executed"
		SOV = {
			country_event = { id = sov_economic.18 days = 1 }
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
		}
	}
}

#Gazprom Media Takeover
country_event = {
	id = sov_economic.19
	title = sov_economic.19.t
	desc = sov_economic.19.d
	picture = GFX_sov_event_gazprom_media
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = sov_economic.19.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_economic.19.a executed"
		set_country_flag = SOV_gazprom3_idea_yes 
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = SOV_gazprom2_idea
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = SOV_gazprom2_idea
				add_idea = SOV_gazprom3_idea
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_idea = SOV_gazprom2_idea
				}
			}
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_ideas = SOV_gazprom3_idea
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = sov_economic.22
	title = sov_economic.22.t
	desc = sov_economic.22.d
	picture = GFX_sov_gazprom_cap
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = SOV
		has_completed_focus = SOV_economic_reform_gazprom
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
				neutrality_neutral_communism_are_in_power = yes
			}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_idea = SOV_gazprom1_idea
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.80
			has_idea = SOV_gazprom2_idea
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.5
			has_idea = SOV_gazprom3_idea
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = sov_economic.22.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_economic.22.a executed"
		SOV = {
			set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization = -0.050 }
			modify_capitalization_support = yes
		}
	}
}

	id = sov_economic.27
	title = sov_economic.27.t
	desc = sov_economic.27.d
	picture = GFX_sov_rosneft_cap
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		original_tag = SOV
		has_completed_focus = SOV_economic_reform_rosneft
		NOT = {
			OR = {
				emerging_communist_state_are_in_power = yes
				neutrality_neutral_communism_are_in_power = yes
			}
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_idea = SOV_rosneft_idea1
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.80
			has_idea = SOV_rosneft_idea2
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.5
			has_idea = SOV_rosneft_idea3
		}
	}

			set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization = -0.075 }

		}
	}

					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization = -1.010 }
			modify_capitalization_support = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				owns_state = 688
			}
			688 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = fossil_powerplant
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization = -1.010 }
			modify_capitalization_support = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { modify_economic = 1 }
		modify_economic_support = yes
	}
}

	id = sov_economic.53
	title = sov_economic.53.t
	desc = sov_economic.53.d
	picture = GFX_sov_event_evraz

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = sov_economic.53.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_economic.53.a executed"
		set_country_flag = SOV_evraz_idea1_yes 
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = SOV_evraz_idea
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = SOV_evraz_idea
				add_idea = SOV_evraz_idea1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_idea = SOV_evraz_idea
				}
			}
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_ideas = SOV_evraz_idea1
			}
		}	
		set_temp_variable = { modify_economic = 1 }
		modify_economic_support = yes
		set_temp_variable = { modify_capitalization = -0.250 }
		modify_capitalization_support = yes
	}
}

		modify_economic_support = yes
		if = {
			limit = {

				check_variable = { kaliningrad_invest < 5 }
			}
			642 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 3
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}

					type = fossil_powerplant
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { kaliningrad_invest > 6 }
				check_variable = { kaliningrad_invest < 5 }
			}

				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 3

					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				add_building_construction = {

					instant_build = yes
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { kaliningrad_invest < 2 }
			}
			642 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			}

					level = 1

				}

					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { kaliningrad_invest > 4 }
				check_variable = { kaliningrad_invest < 3 }
			}
			642 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
				add_building_construction = {

country_event = {
	id = sov_boriska.13
	title = sov_boriska.13.t
	desc = sov_boriska.13.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_russo_usa_forum

	option = {
		name = sov_boriska.13.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_boriska.13.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 15.00 }
		modify_international_investment_effect = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = USA }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = USA }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = SOV }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = industrial_complex
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
			OR = {
				is_in_home_area = yes
				NOT = {
					owner = {
						any_owned_state = {
							free_building_slots = {
								building = industrial_complex
								size > 0
								include_locked = yes
							}
							is_in_home_area = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
		add_building_construction = {
			type = industrial_complex
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = offices
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
				OR = {
					is_in_home_area = yes
					NOT = {
						owner = {
							any_owned_state = {
								free_building_slots = {
									building = offices
									size > 0
									include_locked = yes
								}
								is_in_home_area = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = fossil_powerplant
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = offices
					size > 0
					include_locked = yes
				}
				OR = {
					is_in_home_area = yes
					NOT = {
						owner = {
							any_owned_state = {
								free_building_slots = {
									building = offices
									size > 0
									include_locked = yes
								}
								is_in_home_area = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}

			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = fossil_powerplant
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		add_timed_idea = {
			idea = SOV_invest_agreement
			days = 720
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = sov_boriska.27
	title = sov_boriska.27.t
	desc = sov_boriska.27.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	picture = GFX_boriska_roc

	option = {
		name = sov_boriska.27.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_boriska.27.a executed"
		add_ideas = SOV_state_religion
		SOV_up_3_separatism_in_regions = yes
		if = {
					limit = { has_idea = international_bankers }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = international_bankers
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = small_medium_business_owners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = small_medium_business_owners
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = industrial_conglomerates }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = industrial_conglomerates
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = landowners }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = landowners
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = farmers }
						swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = farmers
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = oligarchs }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = oligarchs
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = maritime_industry }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = maritime_industry
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = the_military }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = the_military
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = intelligence_community }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = intelligence_community
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_idea = labour_unions }
					swap_ideas = {
						remove_idea = labour_unions
						add_idea = the_clergy
					}
				}
	}
	option = {
		name = sov_boriska.27.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_boriska.27.b executed"
		add_ideas = SOV_state_secularism
	}
}

		add_ideas = SOV_european_free_zone
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
			}
			dismantle_faction = yes
		}
		else = {
			leave_faction = yes
		}
		SOV = { add_to_faction = PREV }
		add_ideas = EEP_member
		SOV = { country_event = { id = sov_boriska.10 } }
		leaving_EU = yes
		set_temp_variable = { percent_change = 5 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_index = SOV }
		set_temp_variable = { influence_target = ROOT }
		change_influence_percentage = yes
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				check_variable = { THIS.eurosceptic > 0.5 }
				add = 20
			}
			modifier = {
				check_variable = { THIS.eurosceptic > 0.25 }
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				check_variable = { THIS.eurosceptic < 0.1 }
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_non_aggression_pact_with = SOV
				add = 5
			}
			modifier = {
				is_guaranteed_by = SOV
				add = 5
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				sender_influence_higher_40 = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				add = -10
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value < 0
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 50
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				add = 10
				has_opinion = {
					target = SOV
					value > 100
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				has_government = democratic
				add = 10
			}
			modifier = {
				has_government = nationalist
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sov_boriska.30.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: sov_boriska.28.a executed"
		SOV = { country_event = { id = sov_boriska.11 } }
		ai_chance = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				check_variable = { THIS.eurosceptic > 0.5 }
				add = -20
			}
			modifier = {
				check_variable = { THIS.eurosceptic > 0.25 }
				add = -10
			}
			modifier = {
				check_variable = { THIS.eurosceptic < 0.1 }
				add = 10
			}
		}
	}
}
